Window-level pilot conditions

49 prepared Chinese comprehension questions · local drafts · 12 November 2026

Compare the current window layout with the proposal that shifts 11 excerpts and keeps every prepared question. Each numbered condition targets its assigned percentage of clean content characters in the displayed three verses, within two percentage points. A grammar edit counts the full affected clause. Clean and word-by-word excerpts use the frozen texts.

Edits were selected without using the question or answer key. For each window, the 30% omission and mistranslation selections include their respective 15% selections. Each selection preserves some text in all three verses. “Within target” describes the measured dose. The combined text and the effect on the answer evidence still require review before participant use.

1 Kings 13:1–13:3

谁奉耶和华的话从犹大来？

145 clean content characters.

Clean

13:1 忽然，耶罗波安站在祭坛旁烧香的时候，有一个神人奉耶和华的话，从犹大来到伯特利。

13:2 他奉耶和华的话向祭坛呼喊说：“祭坛哪，祭坛哪！耶和华如此说：‘大卫家必生一个儿子，名叫约西亚。他必在你上面，将那些在你上面烧香的丘坛祭司献为祭，也必在你上面焚烧人的骨头。’”

13:3 当天，神人给了一个预兆，说：“这是耶和华所说的预兆：‘这祭坛必裂开，坛上的灰必倾撒出来。’”

Clean
